# Skyreach API Catalog

_Single-file catalog generated from TypeDoc JSON. Edits will be overwritten._


## cli-kit/src

- `function askLine(rl: Interface, message: string): Promise&lt;string&gt;` — Ask a single line via the provided readline.Interface. Handles Ctrl+C as cancel, restoring prior SIGINT behavior.
- `type Constructor = (args: any[]) =&gt; T;`
- `function error(message: string): void`
- `function info(message: string): void`
- `function isInteractive(): boolean`
- `function makeExitMapper(pairs: [Constructor&lt;unknown&gt;, number][], defaultCode: number): (err: unknown) =&gt; number` — Creates a mapping function that returns an exit code for a given error.
- `function maybeOverride(label: string, computed: T, opts?: MaybeOverrideOpts&lt;T&gt;): Promise&lt;T&gt;` — Show a computed value and let the user optionally override it. Enter = accept computed. Ctrl+C = cancel (throws).
- `function selectFromFiles(files: { label: string; value: string }[], opts?: { autoSelectSingle?: boolean; rl?: Interface; title?: string }): Promise&lt;string&gt;` — Pick a file from a list. If exactly one, auto-select (unless disabled). Uses numbered menu on the existing REPL.
- `function spinner(text: string): { stop: (msg?: string) =&gt; void }` — Minimal spinner for brief work.
- `function usage(message: string): void`
- `class UserCanceledError`
- `function warn(message: string): void`

- `type CalendarConfig = any;`
- `function compareHexCoords(a: HexCoord, b: HexCoord): number` — Compare two hex coordinates for sorting. Sorts by column first (west to east), then by row (north to south). Returns negative if a < b, positive if a > b, 0 if equal.
- `function compareHexIds(a: string, b: string, notation: "letter-number" | "numeric"): number` — Compare two hex ID strings for sorting. Parses to coordinates and compares column-first, then row.
- `function compareSeasonIds(a: string, b: string): number` — Compare two season IDs (e.g., '1511-autumn') for chronological order. Returns -1 if a < b, 1 if a > b, 0 if equal. Sorts by year, then by season order (winter < spring < summer < autumn).
- `type CoordinateNotation = z.infer&lt;CoordinateNotationSchema&gt;;`
- `interface CubeCoord` — Cube coordinates for hex distance calculations. In cube coordinates, x + y + z = 0 always holds.

Always returns lower-case.

- `type DetailTables = Record&lt;Season, DetailTable&gt;;`
- `function displayHexId(id: string, notation: "letter-number" | "numeric"): string` — Format a hex ID for display (uppercase for letter-number). Used in CLI output and user-facing displays.
- `type EffectsTable = Record&lt;WeatherCategory, WeatherEffects&gt;;`
- `function expandHexSpecs(specs: unknown, config: { grid: { columns: number; labelFont: string; notation: "letter-number" | "numeric"; rows: number }; icons: Record&lt;string, { file: string; size: number }&gt;; layers: { defaultVisible: boolean; key: string; label: string; scopes?: "gm:view" | "player:view" | "public:view"[] }[]; outOfBounds: string[]; tagIcons: { fill?: string; icon: string; layer: string; size?: number; stroke?: string; strokeWidth: number; tag: string }[] }): HexSpecResult` — Expand hex specs into individual hex IDs, using the map config for notation, grid bounds and out-of-bounds hexes.  Spec forms (letter-number / numeric notation):   - single hex:  `K7`      / `1107`   - rectangle:   `F10:J14` / `0610:1014`  every column × every row between the corners   - radius:      `M25+2`   / `1325+2`     every hex within N steps of the center   - exclusion:   any form prefixed with `-`  The result is (union of included specs) minus (union of excluded specs), so the order of specs never matters.  Hexes you type (single IDs, rectangle corners, radius centers) must be within the grid; ranges are then clipped to the grid and skip out-of-bounds hexes. A single hex that is marked out-of-bounds is an error.  Errors are collected instead of thrown so a validator can report every bad spec and a renderer can still use the good ones.
- `type ForecastModifierTable = Record&lt;WeatherCategory, number&gt;;`
- `function formatDate(d: null | { day: number; month: "Primaris" | "Gelidus" | "Hibernis" | "Vernalis" | "Pluvoris" | "Florara" | "Solinus" | "Aestara" | "Lucidus" | "Fructara" | "Umbraeus" | "Aridus"; year: number }): string`
- `function formatHexId(coord: HexCoord, notation: "letter-number" | "numeric"): string` — Format internal coordinates as a hex ID string. Always outputs lowercase for letter-number notation.
- `function getDaylightCapForSeason(season: Season): number`
- `function getDaylightCapSegments(date: { day: number; month: "Primaris" | "Gelidus" | "Hibernis" | "Vernalis" | "Pluvoris" | "Florara" | "Solinus" | "Aestara" | "Lucidus" | "Fructara" | "Umbraeus" | "Aridus"; year: number }): number` — Get the daylight cap for a given date, converted to segments (0.5h increments).
- `function getHexesWithinDistance(center: HexCoord, distance: number): HexCoord[]` — Get all hex coordinates within a given distance. Does not filter by grid bounds. Includes the center hex when distance >= 0.
- `function getHexNeighbors(hex: string, notation: "letter-number" | "numeric", mapConfig?: { grid: { columns: number; labelFont: string; notation: "letter-number" | "numeric"; rows: number }; icons: Record&lt;string, { file: string; size: number }&gt;; layers: { defaultVisible: boolean; key: string; label: string; scopes?: "gm:view" | "player:view" | "public:view"[] }[]; outOfBounds: string[]; tagIcons: { fill?: string; icon: string; layer: string; size?: number; stroke?: string; strokeWidth: number; tag: string }[] }): string[]` — Get neighboring hexes for a given hex in a flat-topped hex grid. Uses even-q offset with 0-indexed columns (A=0), where columns A, C, E... are shifted down.
- `function getNeighborCoords(coord: HexCoord): HexCoord[]` — Get all neighboring hex coordinates. Does not filter by grid bounds—caller should filter if needed. Returns exactly 6 neighbors (some may have negative coordinates).
- `function getPerimeterEdges(hexIds: string[], notation: "letter-number" | "numeric"): PerimeterEdge[]` — Return the edges that lie on the boundary of a group of hexes.  An edge is a boundary edge iff the neighbor across it is NOT in the group; out-of-bounds / off-grid neighbors count as "not in the group". This naturally handles disjoint blobs (each contributes its own ring) and interior holes (the ring around a hole is emitted, facing inward). The result is a set: which edges it contains does not depend on the order of `hexIds`.  Membership is tested in coordinate space rather than on formatted hex IDs, so neighbor coordinates that fall off the grid — including negative ones — are handled without round-tripping through `formatHexId` (which rejects negatives).

- `function parseTrailId(trailId: string, notation: "letter-number" | "numeric"): null | { from: string; to: string }` — Parses a trail ID into its constituent hex IDs.
- `interface PerimeterEdge` — A single edge on the boundary of a group of hexes.  `edge` is an index 0–5 into the direction order returned by getNeighborCoords: edge `i` faces the same direction as that function's `i`-th neighbor (NW, N, NE, SE, S, SW on this even-q flat-topped grid). The web layer projects each `(hexId, edge)` to a pixel line segment using a hex-vertex table aligned to this same order, so this ordering is the single source of truth shared by the topology here and the pixel geometry there.

- `function isProdRolloverPath(filePath: string): boolean` — True if the path is a prod rollover file per storage grammar.
- `function isRolloverPath(filePath: string, opts?: { includeDev?: boolean }): boolean` — True if path is either prod or dev rollover file.
- `type LedgerResultsByCharacter = Record&lt;string, { combat: ApDelta; exploration: ApDelta; social: ApDelta }&gt;;`
- `function loadAllYamlInDir(dir: string, validator?: ZodType&lt;T, ZodTypeDef, T&gt;): T[]` — Loads and parses all YAML files in a directory, returning an array of objects of type T. Files ending with .yml or .yaml are included. If a Zod validator is provided, validates each parsed object and warns on failure (with absolute file path).
- `function loadBeats(): Map&lt;string, { campaignStatus: "active" | "inactive"; clues?: string | { context?: string; id: string }[]; factions?: string[]; npcs?: string[]; plotline: string; slug: string; status: "active" | "resolved" | "pending" | "skipped"; tags?: string[]; title: string; trigger?: string }&gt;` — Load every beat under `data/plotlines/`, keyed by canonical `plotlineSlug/beatSlug` ID. Files outside a `beats/` directory (e.g. the plotline's own page) and files that fail validation are skipped. Returns an empty map when the plotlines directory is absent.  Uncached: callers that read this repeatedly in a session (e.g. arrival-alert surfacing) should cache the derived lookup themselves, mirroring the clue loader.
- `function loadHavens(): string[]`
- `function loadMapConfig(): { grid: { columns: number; labelFont: string; notation: "letter-number" | "numeric"; rows: number }; icons: Record&lt;string, { file: string; size: number }&gt;; layers: { defaultVisible: boolean; key: string; label: string; scopes?: "gm:view" | "player:view" | "public:view"[] }[]; outOfBounds: string[]; tagIcons: { fill?: string; icon: string; layer: string; size?: number; stroke?: string; strokeWidth: number; tag: string }[] }` — Load and validate map configuration from map.yaml.
- `function loadMeta(): { nextSessionSeq: number; state: Record&lt;string, { applied?: Record&lt;string, string[]&gt;; backend: "meta" | "ledger" | "external"; checkpoints?: Record&lt;string, string | number | boolean&gt;; fingerprints?: Record&lt;string, string&gt;; mirror?: { info?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; lastMirrorAt?: string } }&gt;; version: 2 }` — Loads `data/meta.yaml` as MetaV2Data. - Accepts v2 (validates) or v1 (migrates → validates). - If migrated, writes back v2 immediately (write-through upgrade).
- `function loadRoleplayBooks(): Map&lt;string, { culturalOverview: string; intelligenceReports?: { instructions?: string; rows: { linkId?: string; linkType?: "hex" | "region" | "dungeon" | "beat" | "clue" | "encounter" | "faction"; relevantConditions: string; report: string; roll: number; sampleDialogue: string }[]; situational?: { linkId?: string; linkType?: "hex" | "region" | "dungeon" | "beat" | "clue" | "encounter" | "faction"; relevantConditions: string; report: string; sampleDialogue: string }[] }; keyword: string; loreHooks: string[]; name: string; pritharaVariants?: { description: string; name: string }[]; rpVoiceNotes: string[]; sampleDialogue: string[] }&gt;` — Load every roleplay book under `data/roleplay-books/`, keyed by file slug (basename without extension). Files that fail validation are skipped. Returns an empty map when the directory is absent.  Uncached: callers that read this repeatedly in a session (e.g. arrival-alert surfacing) should cache the derived lookup themselves, mirroring the clue and beat loaders.
- `function loadRoutesConfig(): objectOutputType` — Load and validate routes.yml from the data directory.
- `function loadSidebarConfig(): { gmOnly: { href?: string; id: string; items: SidebarItemData[]; label: string }[]; shared: { href?: string; id: string; items: SidebarItemData[]; label: string }[] }` — Load and validate sidebar.yml from the data directory.
- `function loadTrails(): Record&lt;string, TrailData&gt;` — Load trails with normalized and sorted IDs.
- `function mapConfigExists(): boolean` — Check if map.yaml exists without throwing an error.
- `function normalizeTrailKeys(trails: Record&lt;string, TrailData&gt;): Record&lt;string, TrailData&gt;` — Normalize all keys to canonical form (e.g., "p12-p13" and "p13-p12" collapse). If duplicates normalize to the same key, the FIRST occurrence wins.
- `function parseBeatFile(raw: string): null | { campaignStatus: "active" | "inactive"; clues?: string | { context?: string; id: string }[]; factions?: string[]; npcs?: string[]; plotline: string; slug: string; status: "active" | "resolved" | "pending" | "skipped"; tags?: string[]; title: string; trigger?: string }` — Parse and validate a single beat file's contents. Returns the validated `BeatData`, or null if the file has no frontmatter, contains malformed YAML, or fails `BeatSchema` validation. Pure — no file I/O — so the parsing and skip rules are testable from raw strings.
- `function parseRoleplayBookFile(raw: string): null | { culturalOverview: string; intelligenceReports?: { instructions?: string; rows: { linkId?: string; linkType?: "hex" | "region" | "dungeon" | "beat" | "clue" | "encounter" | "faction"; relevantConditions: string; report: string; roll: number; sampleDialogue: string }[]; situational?: { linkId?: string; linkType?: "hex" | "region" | "dungeon" | "beat" | "clue" | "encounter" | "faction"; relevantConditions: string; report: string; sampleDialogue: string }[] }; keyword: string; loreHooks: string[]; name: string; pritharaVariants?: { description: string; name: string }[]; rpVoiceNotes: string[]; sampleDialogue: string[] }` — Parse and validate a single roleplay-book file's contents. Returns the validated `RoleplayBookData`, or null if the file contains malformed YAML or fails `RoleplayBookSchema` validation. Pure — no file I/O — so the parsing and skip rules are testable from raw strings.
- `function parseRolloverDevFilename(base: string): null | { seasonId: string }`
- `function parseRolloverFilename(base: string): null | { seasonId: string }`
- `function parseRolloverPath(filePath: string): null | RolloverPathInfo` — Parse a path into rollover info if it matches prod/dev rollover shapes and location.

- `type RolloverInfo = any;` — Internal shape for rollover files on disk.
- `type RolloverPathInfo = any;`
- `function saveMeta(patch: DeepPartial&lt;MetaV2Data&gt;): void` — Saves a partial MetaV2Data to `data/meta.yaml`, deep-merging with existing state. - Arrays in `applied` are union-merged and de-duplicated. - Objects are shallow-merged per field; unspecified subsystems/collections are preserved.
- `function saveTrails(trails: Record&lt;string, TrailData&gt;): void` — Writes the canonical, sorted version of the trails file to disk. Always rewrites the entire file so diffs remain stable.
- `function seasonOfSessionFile(filePath: string): string` — Read the first day_start from a session file and derive a normalized seasonId.
- `const SESSION_FILE_RE: RegExp;`
- `function sortTrailKeys(trails: Record&lt;string, TrailData&gt;): Record&lt;string, TrailData&gt;` — Deterministically sort the map by edge id using hex-aware ordering: first by the first hex (hexSort), then by the second hex (hexSort).
- `function writeJsonl(filename: string, records: Iterable&lt;T&gt;, opts: WriteOpts): void`
- `function writeYamlAtomic(filePath: string, data: any): void` — Write a YAML file atomically.

- `type BeatReferences = z.infer&lt;BeatReferencesSchema&gt;;`
- `const BeatReferencesSchema: ZodOptional&lt;ZodArray&lt;ZodString, "many"&gt;&gt;;` — Beats anchored to a place-feature (landmark or hidden site), mirroring the `clues` field's array style. Elements are bare canonical beat IDs in the `'beat'` LinkType format — `plotlineSlug/beatSlug`, e.g. "istavan-and-the-mask/the-refugees-lament".  The hex owns *where* a beat happens; the beat owns *what* it contains. The per-anchor orientation (when/how the beat fires) already lives in the beat's own `trigger`, so it is not restated here. The link is one-directional — there is deliberately no reverse `hexes` field on beats; "which hexes anchor this beat" is derived by querying hexes, exactly as clue placements are.

- `type HexSpec = z.infer&lt;HexSpecSchema&gt;;`
- `const HexSpecSchema: ZodEffects&lt;ZodString, string, unknown&gt;;` — Shape-only check for a hex spec. The schemas package does not know the campaign's map config, so notation, grid bounds and out-of-bounds hexes are checked when the spec is expanded (`expandHexSpecs` in `@achm/core`).  Forms (shown in letter-number / numeric notation):   - single hex:  `K7`      / `1107`   - rectangle:   `F10:J14` / `0610:1014`  (corners in either order)   - radius:      `M25+2`   / `1325+2`     (every hex within 2 of the center)   - exclusion:   any of the above prefixed with `-`

- `function restoreNumericHexId(value: unknown): unknown` — Unquoted numeric-notation hex IDs reach us as YAML numbers with their leading zeros stripped (`0101` → 101, `-0101` → -101). Letter-number IDs always contain a letter and never parse as numbers, so padding an integer back to four-digit CCRR restores exactly what the author typed. Anything that is not an integer is passed through untouched for the string check to reject.
- `type RoleplayBookData = z.infer&lt;RoleplayBookSchema&gt;;`
- `type RoleplayBookReferences = z.infer&lt;RoleplayBookReferencesSchema&gt;;`
- `const RoleplayBookReferencesSchema: ZodOptional&lt;ZodArray&lt;ZodString, "many"&gt;&gt;;` — Roleplay books reminded at a place-feature (landmark or hidden site), mirroring the `beats` field's array style. Elements are bare book slugs — the `data/roleplay-books/<slug>.yml` filename, e.g. "fort-dagaric".  This is the *place-arrival* trigger for a book: arriving at the feature surfaces the book as a reminder. Unlike beats, the link carries no per-anchor trigger and no status — books are always-relevant reference material, surfaced whole as a pointer (the GM opens the book and chooses what to deliver). The link is one-directional — there is deliberately no reverse `hexes` field on books; "which hexes remind this book" is derived by querying hexes.

These files are written to `data/session-logs/rollovers/` after applying a rollover (either manual via `scribe rollover` or automatic inter-session).  They serve as an audit trail of what happened during the rollover: - Which trails were maintained (near havens) - Which trails persisted (used this season or lucky dice roll) - Which trails were deleted (unused and unlucky dice roll) - Before/after state of all affected trails
